# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Scope and evidentiary rules

This document is a present-tense topology of the RH research formalized in HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower. It records live branch architectures, exact open cores, candidate interfaces, shared structure, and terminal no-gos. It is not chronology, research diary, or catalogue of every theorem.

Evidence surface: declarations indexed from current Lean working tree in HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower. Cards and papers are not evidence here. Incomplete intended theorems are not reported as proved.

Lean declaration snapshot cutoff: **2026-08-31 15:45 CEST; committed and indexed working-tree declarations included**.

Status language:

- **Proved** means an unconditional Lean theorem.
- **Endpoint** means a proved implication or equivalence ending in RH; it does not prove its premise.
- **Receiver** means a formal object that detects or classifies off-line zeros after a source law or divisor input is supplied.
- **Producer** means a theorem derived from the literal arithmetic, theta, prime–Gamma, Möbius, or geometric source before selecting a Xi divisor.
- **Live branch label** means source, connector chain, and RH endpoint are formal, with a first independent producer still open. It does not mean the branch is mathematically independent of other labels.
- **Candidate interface label** means a novel formal receiver or source fragment exists, but the source handoff or divisor exhaustion needed for a live branch does not. It may be another realization of an existing open problem.
- **Terminal** means Lean rules out the stated producer or proves every surviving premise equivalent to RH.

No route currently proves or disproves RH.

## Executive topology

The ledger retains **28 live branch labels** and **33 candidate interface labels**. These are indexing handles, not 61 disjoint routes. A branch is a path through shared mathematical gates; several labels can be presentations of one open source law. CFI is absorbed into CCM-FU. Its completed-Abel readout, CCM-FU checkpoint curvature, SP-E transition core, and RG weighted Chebyshev mass-error share one exact affine-slope Cesàro high-pass. This common current is a discrete scale connection: its gauge kernel is addition of a tail constant, a prescribed potential reconstructs its history tail uniquely, successive uniform innovations are orthogonal to the old centered history, and anchored variance is an exact positive Dirichlet action. The literal completed Suzuki history realizes that potential, current, and variance exactly; eventual boundedness of innovation birth, local action production, and aligned Gamma energy are the same condition and exactly RH. PPM, PEP, and EWQ-V are terminal. RG is a live Gaussian arithmetic/Suzuki-transport architecture. MSC is live because one contractive Sonin schedule crosses the singular Euler target with coefficient-faithful affine correction and exact Ward landing. RG-HF and RG-ETA are subordinate finite-Hardy and eta-current fronts. TMD is a compact prime-modular descent candidate, not an independent producer.

Live branch labels: CCM-FU, C1, CDC, D2, E, F2, STC, CSB, G, G-CE, MSC, L, O, P, Q, CLG/TTF, HB, FZZ-P, PW-B, SP-E, SCE, T1, T2, U, HC, LI, RG, SV2.

RG starts entirely from literal von Mangoldt and archimedean sources. Its Gaussian expression is unconditionally the canonical multiplicity-aware symmetric zero sum, obeys exact heat convolution, and is nonnegative on unbounded widths exactly under RH. Its pointwise Suzuki expression has full holomorphic/Lerch realization, exact hinge and Legendre transport, and a closed first-tail barrier. The pointwise order coordinate is also the exact weighted Chebyshev mass-error Cesàro high-pass driving CCM-FU/CFI and SP-E, up to explicit bounded archimedean and terminal corrections. Integrating the terminal correction gives a divisor-free scale potential whose monotone Dirichlet energy has increment proportional to the completed-Abel square; eventual boundedness of those increments is exactly RH. Exponential rather than bounded control now has a quantitative endpoint: a critical-Mellin resolvent bound `O(exp(alpha*t))`, for `0≤alpha<1/2`, analytically continues the regularized defect and confines every Xi zero to `Re rho≤1/2+alpha`. The complete fixed-contact hierarchy is classified. Eventual boundedness of `alignedEnergy^m/transitionCore^(m-1)` gives width `alpha_m=(m-1)/(2m)`. Order one is exactly RH; every fixed `m≥2` has strictly positive width and cannot manufacture a uniform Green bound from its algebraic inequalities. Quadratic contact is the special width `1/4`, hence only `Re rho≤3/4`. These are exact width receivers, not producers. Even a nonnegative monotone sublinear sequence under the square-root PNT envelope can have high-pass tending to positive infinity, so magnitude envelopes, positivity, monotonicity, and sublinearity do not imply the required order. Open content is signed arithmetic cancellation: a uniform Gaussian discrepancy budget on unbounded widths or control of this common pointwise high-pass through every remaining prefix and compact seam.

FD2 is terminal as trace-faithful polarization. GTA gives a sharp positive quartic classifier on actual theta Schur stages, but actual skew-adjoint energy has a strictly positive limit; only source-relative transverse control survives. S6F/TCD prove global strict reflected second--sixth curvature, every Bell/Fock order-eight connector, positivity of the literal eighth likelihood jet, and native rank-three order-eight curvature at the modular seam and for `x ≥ 137/2000`. On `0<x<137/2000`, Lean confines the actual trajectory to a refined parabolic cone and equips its source-positive vector field with an exact `sl₂` triple. The remaining finite sign has an exact Jacobi--Krein reduction to one theta-specific null-line crossing/intrinsic ninth-jet shift. Generic positive Hankel-head geometry cannot orient it. Neither can regular algebra-valued contravariance: in any nontrivial involutive algebra, both canonical sides fail whenever every nonzero diagonal value is left regular. Every algebra-valued carrier therefore exposes a nonzero vector with left-zero-divisor diagonal pairing. This subsumes scalar, finite matrix, and bounded Hilbert-operator channels. The finite gate is direct orientation of singular-face flux, followed by cofinal/all-order landing; a contravariant survivor must use diagonal zero divisors, be singular/indefinite, or abandon this pairing mechanism.

The exact physical real-slice Fermi velocity has a double pole at every Xi zero, so it has no holomorphic full-strip extension; holomorphic horizontally stratified strip fields are affine and cannot match its hard-edge divergence. If the downstream observer is injective on the image of the full family join, the upstream-to-image chain map and every homology map are isomorphisms; a fixed finite family also vanishes above its arity and has zero top connecting rank. The canonical free-intervention presentation adds the exact packaging law: a proposed route descends through legal rewrites iff its primitive values annihilate every declared relation, and the quotient observer recovers the original reports. Its unquotiented two-field refinement proves more sharply that a unique chain-level route representation exists exactly when both complete defect fields vanish, and reconstructs every global defect as a finite linear combination of primitive boundary and observer defects. There is no hidden higher linear coherence obstruction. These are sharp generic selection and provenance receivers, but no Lean theorem supplies the required fillings, primitive square identities, or relations from the completed arithmetic source. Thus extra finite legs, faithful serial observation, scalar endpoint closure, abstract sublinear filling, or quotient packaging do not create or erase the missing obstruction; a live completion route needs a nontrivial retained Hodge/connecting class and genuine infinite passage.

A nonconstant clock creates an anchored pair interaction equal to clock contrast times organ contrast, and the complete anomaly signature reconstructs clock rate modulo constant gauge. For the literal completed harmonic-to-square-root rate, one fixed two-state sign organ already gives an interaction exceeding every finite bound, so this clock cannot descend through even the smallest nontrivial marginal quotient. Separately, comparison-jet group formation no longer needs a separately supplied determinant premise: tensor flip constructs two-sided inverse differential points, including inverse-adjoint stability on the determinant-one part, and every invertible differential--difference system has a parameterized Picard--Vessiot quotient. With spanning tangent, Lean proves full `SL₂` Kolchin closure or one common nonzero scalar linear differential operator on every group entry; finite-type and jet-adjoin endgames strengthen this to full closure or a base connection. Separately, one source-flow equality would kill the Hedenmalm horizontal carrier, force two-prime Gamma naturality, flatten Route R's corona, and annihilate the Gamma/co-Poisson defect—but that equality is globally RH-equivalent. ### Primitive open gates

| Gate | One underlying mathematical question |
|---|---|
| `ORD` | Can the literal source prove the required signed order, passivity, cancellation, or current budget without absolute-value or divisor-fitted replacement? |
| `COF` | Can one source-selected cofinal family control every ground, escape, scale, aperture, or moving-degree direction rather than one finite packet or trial state? |
| `LAND` | Can source coefficients, multiplicity, prime/depth labels, and orientation survive the required singular boundary, closed-graph, determinant, or physical landing? |
| `ORIENT` | Can the source produce the reflection-odd, off-diagonal, noncentral, higher-compound, or order-sensitive incidence erased by scalar and diagonal receivers? |
| `COMP` | Can a relation proved on finite stages descend through genuine infinite completion, ancestry, or semantic identification without becoming copywise RH? |
| `DYN` | Can the full nonlinear physical source produce the selective scattering law, rather than a target-fed pole, equilibrium, or kinematic classifier? |

These gates are coupled, not a partition. `LAND` is the source/receiver firewall; `ORD`, `COF`, and `ORIENT` are the three dominant mathematical burdens; `COMP` is the infinite-limit form of the firewall; `DYN` is the physical specialization of `LAND + ORIENT`.

At coarsest honest resolution they form two coupled problems:

1. **Source production:** derive a quantitative reflection-sensitive normal datum from literal source (`ORD + ORIENT`, or `DYN`).
2. **Global faithful propagation:** carry that datum through every required scale, state, and completion without loss or copywise fitting (`COF + LAND + COMP`).

Most labels change only carrier used for one of these two problems. A mathematically independent route would need a genuinely different source producer or a genuinely different global propagation theorem, not another exact receiver.

For every finite spectrum, completed-time projective phase law is Haar on the orbit closure and the small-target return-occupation exponent plus exact balanced-relation rank equals level count minus one. Thus return frequency measures relation-space dimension, not horizontal Xi orientation. An explicit nine-level homometric pair has identical scalar return laws for every clock but different projective relation lattices; resolved replicas separate them. Lean also constructs, for every size and qudit dimension, one bounded-degree nearest-neighbour Hamiltonian with a literal noncommuting onsite/edge pair that obeys the exact first-return exponent simultaneously for every finite observer chosen afterward; Hilbert--Schmidt, squared nuclear, nuclear-radius, and pure-state specializations are exact. These theorems have no Xi occurrence or arithmetic selector. Universal recurrence, all-observer validity, occupation exponent, and stationary support are generic, not new RH branches; any handoff must retain resolved characters and acquire literal Xi occurrence.

Generic homological provenance has a sharp nonerasure criterion. Closed coframes that extend through a target map prevent every nonzero finite marked-prime combination from becoming a target boundary and make the transported marked cycles linearly independent modulo boundaries. Flatness and split prime coordinates alone do not suffice: an explicit zero-connection, completely split carrier with identity boundary makes every marked basis vector and every finite combination exact. Thus L/R/JAS-style descent must construct source-derived extending coframes through completion; abstract flatness, splitting, or exactness can erase all prime provenance. This is a connector/no-go theorem, not an RH branch.

Coefficient-dominated finite refinements now have an exact collapse/prism theory: deletion to a presentation core is a homology equivalence, natural under coefficient maps, serial quotient descent, and relabelling, and arbitrary finite strings of legal refinements preserve homology. Legality explicitly requires the deleted mark to be forgettable. Hence dominated forgettable refinements cannot create the missing L/R/JAS obstruction. Conversely, Lean gives a sharp three-leg countermodel: adding a third non-contained marked line can leave the total carrier join unchanged while increasing degree-zero charge from one to two. A successor may gain information only through a non-forgettable independent incidence leg, not presentation refinement alone. No arithmetic specialization or Xi landing makes this a new route.

Generic compact--cofinal observability has an equally sharp essential-margin law. On an infinite-dimensional source, a compact reporter jointly bounded below with a cofinal reporter forces that cofinal reporter to have positive distance from every compact operator. A scalar multiple of the cofinal defect is compact exactly at scalar zero, and retaining the structural carrier preserves the joint lower margin for every defect value. Therefore compact correction, unitary re-coordination, or scalar defect extinction cannot erase a genuinely observed cofinal channel. L/R/STC/O-style compact landings must either change the structural carrier or prove the defect scalar zero from source.

Generic closed-operator packaging is also complete: one compact resolvent canonically yields a bounded analytic Fredholm pencil, and vanishing of a Jordan-window Riesz range, absence of spectrum, pencil invertibility, determinant nonvanishing, and zero boundary winding are equivalent under the stated window hypotheses. This supplies a reusable receiver for C1/K-RVM/W1C; it does not construct an arithmetic closed operator or its source occurrence.

### Oscillatory window capacity and its selector boundary

The corpus prime number theorem now proves `PrimeWindowSupply L` for every `L>0`. At every positive frequency and above every prime cutoff there are primes with sine phase at least `sqrt(2)/2` and primes with sine phase at most `-sqrt(2)/2`. Consequently every interior positive-height mirror-tilt walk has arbitrarily large strictly positive and strictly negative single-prime steps. This removes the former low-frequency restriction from window steering, but it also proves that existence of phase-aligned primes supplies capacity in both orientations and therefore cannot select the critical wall or one side of it. Any good-scale set whose complement is smaller must meet this valley. This is an exact generic coverage engine for persistent single-frequency anomalies, but no Lean theorem yet identifies such a mode and exceptional-set bound with a coefficient-faithful RH source channel. It is route-neutral support, not a new branch.

The literal mollified direct head has a positive arbitrary-window mean-square upper form. The full long base moment has a sharp Selberg-diagonal approximation and explicit harmonic/logarithmic main term; the underlying Barban--Vehov Selberg diagonal has normalized limit one. The omitted-head boundary is reduced to a finite explicit arithmetic core. Its base, cross, and derivative forms are respectively one, three, and eight min-log monomials. Horizontal layers converge projectively to Cauchy-resolvent multipliers; finite products converge in operator norm to sums of rank-one leakage channels, with finite exponential Hankel transport. Leakage norm is bounded by multiplier norm and bounded below by evaluation at every denominator zero. ## Terminal and retired routes

Closure applies to the named mechanism, not to every future architecture using related objects.

### FD2 — signed Xi occurrence and complete polarization lock

Literal infinite Hedenmalm theta source admits source-selected trace-corrected signed finite truncations. Their ordinary determinants equal trace-normalized Hilbert--Carleman determinants, converge locally uniformly to normalized Xi, and eventually carry exact Xi zero multiplicity on every zero-free bounded aperture. This coefficient-faithful occurrence theorem survives for reuse.

The approximation interface itself is completely classified as nonselective. Traceless finite-support kernels with a nonreal ordinary Fredholm divisor are Hilbert--Schmidt dense around every Hilbert--Schmidt kernel on an infinite carrier. Applied to the literal theta source, every accuracy admits a stage satisfying all four recorded approximation properties—finite support, Hilbert--Schmidt regularity, prescribed error, and zero diagonal trace—while carrying a nonreal divisor. This does not assert that the chosen source stage is bad; it proves those four properties cannot imply finite-stage reality or Green neutrality.

The natural missing flag is also completely identified. Exact complete Green neutrality on every nonzero mode of every actual theta stage is equivalent to all-stage real divisors and, together with unconditional Hilbert--Schmidt/compact-open completion, implies RH. A root-free formulation uses only actual finite-stage matrix power traces and real-polynomial coefficients: for an `n`-point stage, nonnegativity through degree `2n+1` already forces every Schur node real without spectral weights, root enumeration, or conjugation closure. This finite-window stage property is equivalent to unrestricted Hermite positivity and modal Green neutrality; its cofinal flag closes RH. These are sharp receiver theorems, not upstream source lemmas. A successor must derive them from a strictly weaker arithmetic law or use a different orientation interface.

A genuinely source-derived real-stage interface does not assume this flag. The parabolic moment is defined literally from rational--Gamma mass minus the von Mangoldt contribution, with no Xi divisor coordinate. Its degree-matched Hankel blocks are positive definite, every finite trace-Hankel block is positive semidefinite, and Gauss quadrature produces exactly `k+1` distinct real nodes. Centering these nodes gives a canonical trace-zero diagonal stage, so the required trace-Hankel block indexed by `0..2n+1` is positive semidefinite unconditionally. This does not contradict the FD2 lock: no theorem identifies the centered arithmetic-Gauss determinants with ordinary theta stages or proves their compact-open convergence to Xi. The construction produces real finite spectra by dropping the occurrence/landing theorem. The exact open interface is coefficient-faithful determinant identification or compact-open Xi landing for these source stages.

Every trace-faithful polarization mechanism is classified. Fixed/convergent metrics have a nonzero limiting adjoint defect. Positive escaping source-Gram/Hermitian dilation gives real finite divisors but changes the second jet and converges to a function provably different from Xi. Genuine hyperbolic Krein doubling preserves every holomorphic source power trace, but its determinant is source times Schwarz reflection and is real-divisor exactly when the source divisor is. Signed traces only through total even rank already force determinant factorization. Consequently a real-divisor trace-faithful graded lift proves RH even if its odd determinant has poles; compact pole escape cannot substitute for orientation.

At the actual coefficient-faithful finite theta stages, canonical-metric defect has an exact source/Stokes decomposition: a fixed nonzero rank-two pinned Green current plus a correction commutator tending to zero in operator norm. Complete stage Green forms converge to the pinned form. Two fixed source vectors retain quadratic values above `1/12` and below `-1/12` eventually, so the stages remain more than `1/13` in operator norm from both positive and negative cones. Eventual canonical-metric adjointness or semidefinite approximation is impossible. On every stage eigenmode, Green charge is exactly `-2 Im(mu)` times canonical metric energy. This is a sharp boundary classifier, not a producer of neutrality.

GTA's negative-Pell graded family gives positive quartic charge, invariant under conjugation/reindexing and zero exactly for real packets. Schur transport closes finite landing on actual theta stages. Every off-real Xi zero gives one positive reciprocal-charge floor for every Schur presentation of every sufficiently late stage; hence cofinal smallness forces RH. Conversely, under RH, one uniform Hilbert--Schmidt ceiling bounds all stages, compact-open determinant convergence excludes bounded non-small transversely separated Schur nodes, and the dimension-free split forces charge to zero uniformly over every Schur realization. Therefore RH is exactly uniform late-stage GTA charge decay.

The root-free full-defect producer is terminal. `HS²-Re tr(M²)` is exactly half ordinary skew-adjoint Hilbert--Schmidt energy and decomposes into nonnormal Schur energy plus twice squared imaginary spectral mass. Quartic charge is bounded by `(575/2) HS²` times skew-adjoint energy. On actual corrected theta stages this energy has an explicit coefficient-only formula, but it converges to the strictly positive skew-adjoint energy of the fixed theta source inverse and eventually exceeds half that limit. Cofinal asymptotic Hermiticity is impossible. A real-spectrum Jordan block independently shows the full defect is not necessary for zero GTA charge. The strongest survivor is an exact positive limiting nonnormality law. Any GTA successor must isolate transverse spectral energy relative to that persistent component, or justify a source-relative subtraction before Schur landing. The known RH-to-charge-decay proof remains downstream; full-defect decay, current approximation, entrywise summation, and semidefinite-cone approximation are terminal. Arithmetic Gauss staging remains complementary but lacks Xi landing. FD2 remains terminal.

## Cross-route conclusions

### 1. Source/receiver firewall

Most routes possess a sharp RH-equivalent receiver. Rewriting it as positivity, passivity, flatness, determinant convergence, bounded energy, KMS exchange, or spectral completeness does not advance RH unless Lean derives the premise from literal source before choosing a zero. RG crosses more of this firewall than most labels: arithmetic formula, zero-sum landing, heat propagation, pointwise transport, and the first tail barrier are unconditional. CCM-FU/CFI/SP-E cross it jointly: one completed Abel source lands coefficient-faithfully in Cartan, Gamma, and Xi receivers and is exactly RG's weighted mass-error scale connection up to explicit corrections. Its gauge, potential, positive Dirichlet action, monotone completed formation energy, and exponential-growth-to-zero-width continuation are closed. Canonical signed compensation reaches the necessary linear scale but leaves the RH-equivalent aligned residual; fixed affine mixing cannot improve it. Only signed arithmetic order remains. L similarly has source cyclicity and a complete Burnol aperture receiver, but source-to-fixed-physical agreement is already the wall condition. MSC separately reaches the critical Ward coefficient and leaves fixed-four order. FD2 crosses occurrence but not polarization. C1, L, HB, Route R, TMD, and operator/ground routes still stop at source selection rather than receiver construction.

### 2. Local exactness is abundant; global selection is scarce

Finite packets, jets, homology, local indices, multiplicities, and real-zero determinants can be realized without fixing horizontal position. Fixed autonomous order cannot retain cofinally many prime clocks. RG makes cofinal burden exact; its completed formation energy shows that monotonic positive accumulation still leaves RH entirely in bounded production, and the square-root PNT envelope does not provide it. RG-HF leaves reflection residual/phase dispersion, while RG-ETA kills every lower finite-work prefix and leaves one top order-`m-1` coupled term. E supplies another warning: exact Ramaré integer incidence, two-sided Heath--Brown collision/mean-square equivalence, finite Kloosterman direct-sum energy, and reciprocal Gibbs dissipation coexist with a missing moving/inter-modulus cancellation theorem. Exact Feller tensor admission at every depth similarly forces decision effects to escape every equicontinuous gauge. FZZ adds that even a source-native injective two-coordinate report can miss every actual critical-line mode at every physical scale. FD2 occurrence cannot orient its divisor; GTA full skew energy has a positive floor; S6F/TCD leave one theta-specific crossing; LI leaves one radial divided difference. Successful selectors require infinite coefficient provenance plus source-uniform order, not more finite packaging.

Target cardinality is also closed at fixed occupied dimension. One determinant-optimal positive covariance controls capture of an arbitrary bounded, even uncountable, target family with cost equal to occupied dimension times one worst-target quadratic moment; the same law excludes post-ramp re-entry and has a quenched probability form. Thus continuum union bounds and target enumeration are not route cores. U's remaining issue is stronger: the present one-target scalar moment/action package permits exact sparse quadratic escape with summable entries, so only a literal-source cross-degree or history-coupled law can change its frontier.

### 3. Orientation is the common missing datum

Scalar positivity, absolute values, determinants, unitary gauges, positive diagonal completion, and common modular gain repeatedly erase the sign distinguishing a zero from its reflection. Ordinary probability and equal total mass coexist with negative reflected squares and maximal total-variation separation. Regular Hardy Gram geometry is blind to the inner factor. No positive operator can carry the signed centered-Xi divisor through a zero-free-gauge spectral determinant. APF supplies rigid negative winding but completion cannot nucleate charge; HB supplies copywise beat and helicity classifiers; FD2 supplies correct signed Xi occurrence, but positive polarization changes it and exact indefinite polarization cannot orient it. C1 supplies positive nonlocal cross-packet interference, but not its Weil landing. P supplies another real-rooted wrong divisor. For most complexes, remaining problem is coefficient-faithful source orientation of correct occurrence. RG is the important exception: occurrence and orientation are encoded already, and remaining problem is uniform arithmetic order of the literal atomic--continuous discrepancy on a cofinal heat family.

### 4. Pairing is necessary but not sufficient

P, CLG/TTF, Q, CDC, HB, LI, JAS, K-RVM, GTA, APF, S6F, WS2, FZZ-P, RBH, PW-B, WBF, XST, PMP/LPC, terminal NDS, and EGC are paired or multi-channel and nonclassical. P's balanced Hellinger pair has a real but wrong divisor. FZZ's natural radial/phase pair is injective and phase-complete yet fails literal critical-line incidence. One-leg reporters can fail while Hermitian incidence survives, but pairwise Gram data still miss independent third compounds and transverse grades. Higher arity alone is insufficient: an injective downstream observer preserves complete coefficient complex and homology, while the free two-field law proves all global linear defects are generated by primitive boundary and observer defects—there is no hidden higher linear coherence to rescue an invalid square. Nonconstant observer clocks do create a canonical pair interaction anomaly and its full signature reconstructs clock rate modulo constants. The literal completed Riesz clock with one fixed two-state organ already makes that anomaly unbounded, killing marginal descent; what remains is coefficient-faithful Xi landing and signed order of the surviving interaction. PMP/LPC exposes and combines hidden contrast but does not force it to vanish. HB separates covariance from QNM locality and preserves pseudo-unitarity off wall through partner occupation; adaptive all-order clocks cancel every fibre. PW-B, FZZ, RBH balance, and HB clock/chiral amplitude are the same Ward charge up to positive factors. EGC's centered-shift correction is exact pair covariance, but pole-safe source landing remains open. GTA retains nonselective nonnormality beside transverse charge. The survivor must carry a nonzero bioriented connecting class through genuine infinite completion and receive a non-copywise source law controlling it.

### 5. Positive completion can fail before the spectral problem

TD/C1 raw domination fails at zeroth mass. The global pairwise mean-dispersion polarization shows that positive completion remains possible only after changing the centering interface; its completed Weil landing is open. Positive diagonal repair fails at critical jets, and off RH any successful repair must feed divisor-isolating atoms by at least their negative deficit. Positive coefficient-recovering Abel/heat/Cesaro regularization of the trace-one reflection-incidence mass has no endpoint gain: sparse cofinal bounded return is already summability, hence copywise critical-line placement. Route J fails from unavoidable diagonal energy. Positive Fock/det-two determinants fail before occurrence because centered Xi has real zeros of both signs. Repair of a full infinite negative sector cannot be compact or Schatten. These are normalization obstructions, not missing estimates. Viable successors must alter carrier or centering before positivity projection, introduce cancellation before norms, or accept signed, paired, genuinely noncompact, or atom-fed correction.

### 6. Ground and limit order cannot be interchanged

O's full ground problem reduces source-relatively, at arbitrary positive accuracy, to one finite head at each aperture. CLG/TTF has no noncompact seam burden: radial `C₅` and direct-J `Ω` are positive near zero and cofinally, so failure lies in a compact core. The stripped center-velocity core is negative near the seam; the full action core survives only with likelihood curvature, Bregman displacement, and exponential reserve coupled. Direct Jacobian, homotopy, and stationary-chord alternatives remain. Uniformly separated tail packets are positive, so negative escape must cluster. F2 still needs one common zero on its compact zeroth-moment fibre. U admits sparse quadratic escape. APF/UHF has no charge without a finite ancestor; fixed D2/P prefixes fail cofinally; Route R's two completion orders differ. Limit order, moving heads, nonlocal reserves, density across scales, and retained infinite sectors are part of the theorem.

### 7. Singular interfaces remain credible

Regular bounded descent is terminal in L, R, PSRC, PCS, P, JAS, and HDL. Exact one-leg graph reconstruction from all-cross-prime `ell²` packets is discontinuous at zero, and no bounded linear lift exists. Moving bounded diagonal clocks commute asymptotically with Sonin pullback while Route-R residue remains `±1`. TMD adds a compact prime-circle interface where continuous or `L1` descent is already the neutral-rate selector, and nonneutral histories force carry/rank escape. Shared survivor is genuinely singular/noncontinuous, non-diagonal infinite-support completion retaining phase/depth/compound provenance and a nonzero connecting class through Xi landing.

### 8. Dynamic selectors must act before late-time smoothing

HB, FZZ-P, PW-B, and terminal RBH show late equilibrium does not orient displacement. HB's total third-order cokernel is exactly negative source-clock-defect energy; Peierls incidence removes stationary `+-`, leaving radiative `++`. Finite/local/Hermitian/passive/all-pass/QNM-only and regular nonlinear mechanisms are terminal. Even adding a regular threshold is insufficient unless it carries the odd source phase; finite angular order cannot realize the reciprocal `Q+2` response, and local self-dual Jacobi cycling produces positive rigidity rather than darkness. HB therefore needs source-adapted threshold/continuum data, nonlocal or infinite angular response, and full causal Green orientation before projection. FZZ-P cannot impose finite exponential type because its canonical response already has Riemann--von Mangoldt divisor growth; it needs maximal-order-one uniqueness density, changed radial incidence, source deformation, or a singular category. PW-B needs independent boundary calibration. All feed the same Ward receiver.

### 9. Corpus-native nonclassical topology

JAS joins prime-support cubical connection, singular all-grade Mellin ladder, compounds, and tail-Roos persistence. Route R adds noncommuting completion and protected reflection-odd derived class. TMD uses dense compact prime recurrence, integrable cocycle descent, and infinite shell-replica properness. E's Ramaré front is an exact ordinary-integer incidence with conditional-negative prime-load geometry and ternary antiscale escape. RG joins literal atomic--continuous discrepancy, multiplicity-aware zero-sum landing, heat semigroup, pointwise Lerch transport, scale connection, gauge class, and monotone formation action. L joins explicit source cyclicity to a singular physical-reader mismatch. WBF has noncentral Hodge anomaly; HDL a double-log reporter; SC a rigged Krein object; EWJ a negative Euler cubic current; O a source-head ground reduction; LI an all-mode radial law; HB a radiative full-cokernel clock transduction. These are corpus-native mechanisms, not classical-route restatements.

APF has only finite charge ancestors. GTA cannot have full skew-adjoint decay. S6F/TCD leave one singular-face crossing; CLG leaves compact projective/action/Bose gates. HB leaves chiral radiative extinction at one source clock. LI leaves one radial divided difference. E leaves either fixed Hall/co-moving transport or moving Ramaré/Gallagher control; two-sided Heath--Brown comparison, explicit primitive log-window packing, and finite Kloosterman energy do not supply it. T1 needs co-moving/singular prime extraction; T2 a second singular comparison. HCF's lattice alias and second jet are gone, first-order winding is evaluated, and its 75% gate is reduced to one source-defined positive-shift evaluation per height. Its RH-strength core remains compact source-boundary floor `4*pi-4/25`; current real-zero-multiplicity control is only an upper bound, with five-center/hybrid budget as alternate front. AKC's complete recursive integral greedy flag is closed, but Xi lattice membership/profile/contractivity are not. FEL/PMP/LPC lack zero-to-balance and Xi-history landing; generic Feller tensor admission necessarily loses cofinal equicontinuity. TMD lacks source continuity/properness at compact completion.

## Normalized frontier verdict

The corpus does not support treating route labels as independent attempts. It supports overlapping labels built from six primitive gates and seven receiver complexes.

- CCM-FU/CFI, SP-E, RG, G, P, and MSC meet in Ward/boundary receivers. CFI is absorbed into CCM-FU. Completed-Abel, checkpoint-curvature, scale-connection, RG weighted mass-error, and completed formation-energy production are one pre-divisor law; Cartan/Gamma/Xi landing, positive energy geometry, and exponential-growth-to-zero-width continuation are closed. Canonical signed correction supplies the forced linear compensation but fixed affine mixing leaves only the RH-equivalent aligned residual, source erasure, or vacuous sign. The complete fixed-contact ladder has width `(m-1)/(2m)`; `m=1` is exactly RH and every `m≥2` loses positive width, with quadratic contact giving `1/4`. Shared gate is first-contact signed arithmetic `ORD`, not admission or another energy definition. MSC separately closes singular target-`1` passage and leaves fixed-four order. G is one real-channel cap and P one finite ceiling above `233/20`.
- C1, O, Q, CLG/TTF, UEB, SV2, S6F, and EGC are global-selection variants. C1 needs its first-moment identity and Weil landing; O one finite-head inequality and cofinal passage; S6F/TCD one theta-specific crossing. CLG's noncompact radial ends are positive. Its homotopy has closed endpoints, projective-curvature energy, highest-channel cancellation, and two lower velocity centers; the remaining homotopy gate is the explicit lower-Bose compensation on the closed segment. Fixed low-face and shifted positive Lorentz/Hankel cones are terminal. Direct `Ω`, full cubic signed-Bose/coupled-action reserve, and stationary chord are alternate compact gates. EGC needs pole-safe nested landing and cofinal vanishing.
- RG, F2, STC, CSB, U, PMP/LPC, and related candidates are cofinal-selection variants. RG leaves uniform Gaussian discrepancy or bounded local production of the common formation action beyond the first closed pointwise barrier. RG-HF leaves finite-to-entire rigidity; RG-ETA leaves one top order-`m-1` square-root-scale coupling after all lower prefixes vanish. F2 already has a source-selected diffuse-prime zeroth weld and needs simultaneous higher moments. U lacks degree-uniform synchronization. Generic finite Feller tensor realization is exact but every nontrivial cofinal decision family loses equicontinuity, so source-derived resolution control—not finite admission—is the formation gate. Canonical raw-Möbius NB/BD approximation is terminal.
- L, CDC, PSRC, PCS, TCD, JAS, XST, WBF, HDL, TMD, TK, SC, RFHM, and R's survivors are descent variants. L's exact remaining square is source cyclic reader versus fixed physical reader; its Burnol aperture system is complete exactly at aperture at least one but remains zero-fed. Shared core is divisor-independent singular, non-diagonal infinite-support sewing retaining compound/depth/phase provenance and a nonzero connecting class through Xi completion. Bounded cross-prime graph lift and diagonal-clock erasure are terminal. TMD's compact recurrence and `L1` potential are exact receivers; source continuity/properness is open. SC scalar unitization is impossible and unrestricted additive determinants are universal.
- D2, E, G-CE, SP-E, SCE, T1, T2, HC, LI, RG, HCF, EWJ, and related labels are signed-incidence variants. D2 needs one infinite pure-prime lower bound. E has two fronts: fixed Hall/co-moving transport, or moving Gallagher control of its exact Ramaré squarefree integer-pair kernel; marker capacity, finite recombination, two-sided positive sparse mean-square/collision comparison, primitive log-window packing `≤4 delta Q²+1`, and finite-modulus residue Parseval are closed. SP-E and CCM-FU share RG's scale connection and no longer lack source admission or energy geometry. T1 needs co-moving/singular prime extraction; T2 a second singular comparison; HC signed singular landing; LI a radial shape law. HCF has exact Euler-affine Robin-jet landing, alias-free phase cells, endpoint-only second jet, evaluated first-order winding, conservation `covariance+boundary -> 4*pi`, and a single-positive-shift `pi ↔ 3/4` critical-line gate. Its current boundary theorem is an upper real-zero-multiplicity bound, while RH strength needs the lower floor `4*pi-4/25` or alternate hybrid budget. EWJ lacks divisor exhaustion.
- HB, FZZ-P, and PW-B are nonlinear/scattering labels sharing one Ward receiver; RBH is terminal. HB's complete cokernel is exact negative clock-defect energy and physical incidence leaves only radiative `++`. Remaining producer must be source-adapted and phase-odd at threshold, nonlocal/infinite in angular response, and use complete causal continuum orientation; phase-blind thresholds, finite angular polynomials, local Plebanski/Jacobi cycling, and prior finite/local/Hermitian/passive/QNM-only mechanisms are terminal. FZZ-P's scalar fusion and natural joint radial/phase incidence are dead, and finite exponential type excludes its canonical physical response; it needs maximal-order-one uniqueness density, changed radial incidence, source deformation, or singular/non-entire data. PW-B needs independent boundary law.
- K-RVM, GTA, APF, WS2, W1C, IRK, terminal FD2, and operator C1 are occurrence/polarization variants. K-RVM is zero-fed; APF cannot create charge; FD2 is terminal. GTA closes Schur transport and exactly classifies RH by uniform charge decay. Its root-free defect separates nonnormal and transverse energies, but actual full energy converges to a positive limit; full-defect decay is terminal. Arithmetic Gauss lacks Xi convergence; C1 has explicit positive trace-class occurrence geometry and exact first-trace reduction but not the required first scalar identity or full landing; WS2 lacks spectral identity; P has a wrong divisor. Correct occurrence and source orientation must coexist without root fitting or erasing persistent nonnormal source geometry.
- AKC is a candidate, not a live route. One integral determinant-one action now gives a complete recursively primitive-minimal Schur flag and mass-greedy Jacobi decomposition, in addition to real/Hermitian Siegel reduction, determinant balancing, minor selection, and all-place descent. Xi arithmetic membership, source control of invariant determinant profile/target scale, and conjugate-packet contractivity remain absent; integrality alone admits an off-critical unit.
- WBF's conserved source-normal jet and equal-mass/maximal-variation fracture remain the same `ORIENT` invariant seen through Hodge curvature, Stokes excess, modular energy, transverse energy, barcode width, and prime-tail entropy. Full planar LDP now closes the concrete radial model completely. Every radial profile remains reflection-blind. Open content is coefficient-faithful noncentral `LAND + COMP`, shared with L and R descendants.

Consequently, recombining labels is progress only when one source theorem discharges a shared gate across multiple receiver complexes. A renamed composite or another RH-equivalent endpoint is not a route. Corpus supports three strongest producer topologies:

`literal source -> reflection-odd mixed incidence -> coefficient-faithful singular completion -> cofinal whole-state/no-escape control -> existing RH receiver`.

`literal von Mangoldt/Chebyshev source -> [Gaussian discrepancy on unbounded widths | signed control of the scale connection / local formation production plus compact sign] -> [heat propagation | checkpoint/Cartan/Gamma/Xi fission] -> RH`.

`literal squarefree Möbius source -> [fixed Hall relation | exact Ramaré integer-pair incidence] -> moving cofinal cancellation/Gallagher control -> Möbius half-power bound -> RH`.

MSC is the strongest assembled instance of first topology, leaving fixed-four `ORD + ORIENT`; TMD is its compact-recurrence candidate boundary. Second topology merges RG, CCM-FU/CFI, and SP-E at one exact scale connection and monotone formation action, while retaining RG's independent Gaussian front. It now has a full quantitative contact ladder through zero-free width and an explicit unbounded clock-pair interaction, but only first-contact boundedness reaches RH; fixed affine signed compensation and marginal descent are terminal. Its remaining content is signed arithmetic cancellation plus coefficient-faithful use of the interaction, not energy construction or a PNT magnitude envelope. Third topology has exact finite Ramaré incidence, marker supply, two-sided sparse collision/mean-square equivalence, and finite-modulus residue energy but no moving cofinal/inter-modulus cancellation theorem. These are current frontiers: source `ORD/COF/ORIENT`, singular `LAND/COMP`, or nonlinear `DYN`; additional receivers do not change them.
